We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [72] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius是一个在运行时动态切换张量并行(TP)和专家并行(EP)的MoE模型服务系统，无需重启或丢弃请求。在8x H200 GPU上测试Qwen3-235B-A22B，可匹配任一静态并行策略的最优性能，RL rollout提升1.16-1.25倍，切换延迟215-434ms，内存开销仅2.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型服务需多GPU并行，TP适合低并发，EP适合高并发。实际生产负载（如在线服务的突发流量、强化学习rollout的长尾请求）在不同并发级别间动态变化，固定使用任一并行策略会导致性能损失。

Method: 核心洞察：EP和TP是同一模型的两种布局，使用字节相同的专家权重和KV cache，切换仅改变每条Rank拥有哪些分片。Moebius在解码步骤间切换并行策略，保持两种布局的运行时常驻，通过固定地址重分片并使用融合GPU-to-GPU传输内核迁移所有权变更的分片。

Result: 在8x H200 GPU Serving Qwen3-235B-A22B，Moebius在所有运行点匹配更优的静态并行策略；RL rollout中性能提升1.16-1.25倍；切换延迟215-434ms；内存开销仅2.4%。

Conclusion: Moebius通过识别EP/TP本质等价性，实现了高效的运行时并行策略切换，使MoE服务系统能够自适应不同并发特性的工作负载。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [73] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xsim是一个异构感知的分布式LLM训练模拟器，支持非均匀工作负载划分、异构感知的集合通信、流水线并行算法抽象，以及与NS-3/htsim的插件式集成，实验验证预测误差小于5%（数据并行/张量并行）和约2%（流水线并行）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI训练模拟器假设同构计算和网络基础设施，但现实训练基础设施越来越异构，原因包括：(1)多模态和MoE等模型架构通过利用异构性提升设备利用率；(2)公有云平台因硬件快速迭代难以提供足量同构硬件；(3)大型企业部署的地理分布式基础设施天然多样且异构。现有模拟器无法准确模拟这种异构环境下的训练过程。

Method: 提出Xsim模拟器，核心设计包括：(i)通过非均匀工作负载划分实现异构设备组间的负载均衡；(ii)基于自定义环构造和块分区的异构感知集合通信；(iii)为流水线并行算法和非均匀张量重分片技术提供可复用抽象；(iv)灵活的输入抽象，支持自定义设备组和设备到并行映射的部署规范；(v)可插拔集成NS-3和htsim，允许用户在模拟保真度与性能/可扩展性之间权衡。

Result: 评估表明Xsim能准确预测真实异构部署的训练时间：大多数异构数据并行/张量并行配置的误差小于5%，流水线并行通信建模误差约2%。此外还能暴露流水线气泡时间和落后者等待时间等可操作指标。

Conclusion: Xsim能够准确预测异构环境下的训练时间，为分布式LLM训练系统的优化提供了可靠的仿真工具，可帮助研究者识别性能瓶颈并指导异构基础设施的部署决策。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [74] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RolloutPipe是一个用于 disaggregated RLVR 系统的大模型推理后训练框架，通过完整组管道化(CGP)和前沿组调度(FGD)技术，在保持 on-policy 正确性的前提下，将 rollout 生成与训练阶段流水线化执行，显著减少 GPU 空闲等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 RLVR 系统采用 disaggregated 架构分离 rollout 生成和策略训练，但现有同步 GRPO 系统需完成全部 rollout 后才开始训练，导致 trainer GPU 池空闲等待；而异步流水线虽能重叠两阶段，但会产生过时数据问题。

Method: RolloutPipe 提出两项技术：1) 完整组管道化(CGP)：当完整组生成完成后立即 FIFO 调度到 trainer；2) 前沿组调度(FGD)：在 Rollout 节点的准入策略，优先处理构成下一训练批次所需的前沿组请求，使 trainer-ready 的组更早、更稳定地到达。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 模型、四个推理/科学基准测试和十二种 rollout 配置下，RolloutPipe 相比 SOTA 系统 Slime，rollout 到训练结束时间缩短 30.7%-42.3%，trainer 等待率降低 37%-76%。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe 通过流水线化设计在不牺牲 on-policy 正确性的前提下，实现了 disaggregated RLVR 系统中训练阶段的提前启动，有效提升了资源利用率和训练效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [75] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DMuon，一个开源的分布式Muon优化器实现，可在现有训练管道中作为即插即用模块使用，无需修改框架层面。在嵌入式基础模型和LLM训练工作负载上，DMuon实现端到端步骤时间1.48x-3.01x加速，优化器步骤时间6.85x-163.00x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵正交化优化器（如Muon）虽然展现出良好的收敛性能，但其更新耦合整个权重矩阵且需要昂贵的Newton-Schulz迭代，导致朴素实现在前向和后向传播上产生超过2倍的开销。当代分布式训练基础设施围绕元素级优化器设计，与矩阵级优化器不匹配，亟需高效解决方案。

Method: 本文提出DMuon，一个开源分布式Muon实现。该方案将Muon优化器集成到现有训练管道中作为即插即用模块，无需框架级修改。通过分布式优化设计来解决矩阵级更新的耦合问题。

Result: 在嵌入式基础模型和LLM训练工作负载上，DMuon实现端到端步骤时间1.48x-3.01x加速，优化器步骤时间6.85x-163.00x加速，将每步延迟降低至接近AdamW水平，并支持模型训练的高效扩展。

Conclusion: DMuon成功解决了矩阵正交化优化器在分布式训练中的效率问题，使Muon能够在大规模模型训练中高效部署，为未来更大规模异构模型架构的训练提供了可行的优化器方案。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代数据生成流程，通过构建级联线性特征样本来改进激活导向方法的可解释性和可控性，相比简单的二元对比样本对，能更有效地分离特征，并在检测和抑制谄媚行为任务上取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活导向方法需要大量对比样本对来明确展示期望或不期望的行为，但这些样本对的质量决定了可解释性框架检测行为相关特征的可靠性，进而影响模型导向效果。

Method: 提出迭代数据生成流程，利用"级联样本"(cascading samples)超越简单的二元样本对，通过隔离显示与行为线性扩展的特征程度的样本来实现更好的特征解耦。具体聚焦于检测和抑制谄媚行为(sycophancy)——即语言模型优先考虑用户验证的倾向。

Result: 证明通过级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分离子空间，能够选择比基线方法更清晰对应期望行为的模型激活。该方法在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒导向任务上与或优于LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时具有更低计算成本和更强可解释性保证。

Conclusion: 级联样本迭代数据生成流程能有效隔离与行为相关的级联线性特征，为检测和抑制语言模型谄媚行为提供了更可靠的激活导向方法。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [77] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基准测试精度饱和后不应简单废弃，而应从构建效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型vs脚手架相对重要性、人机协作提升等六个维度评估智能体，并以CORE-Bench Hard为案例研究发现，即使精度饱和，基准仍可有效衡量其他关键性能维度，同时发现人机协作可实现约2倍的统计显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试精度饱和后通常被废弃替换，但这种做法过度偏好准确性，忽视了智能体性能的其他六个关键维度，包括构建效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架的相对重要性以及人机协作提升。

Method: 以计算可重复性基准CORE-Bench Hard为案例研究：(1)识别构建效度威胁并改进为v1.1版本，创建分布外任务集CORE-Bench OOD；(2)在精度饱和后仍通过该基准测量效率、可靠性、模型和脚手架性能；(3)开展小规模随机实验评估人机协作提升。

Result: 发现CORE-Bench Hard存在难以预见的构建效度威胁；精度饱和后基准仍可用于测量效率、可靠性等维度；人机协作实验发现约2倍的统计显著加速（可能低估，因五分之一的人工作业超时未完成）。

Conclusion: 提出了比当前以准确性为中心的评估范式更严格的替代方案，证明基准饱和后仍可通过多维度评估获得有价值的智能体性能洞察。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [78] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，大语言模型中的拒绝行为与角色特征并非独立机制，而是存在相互作用：顺从的角色特征会"门控"拒绝行为。通过在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B模型上进行方向干预实验，发现顺从角色引导可将Llama的拒绝率从97%降至2%，而重新引入拒绝方向仅在晚层部分恢复拒绝能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将拒绝行为和角色特征作为独立的线性方向分别研究，忽视了它们之间可能的交互作用。这一研究空白导致对模型行为机制的理解不完整。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct两个模型中提取"顺从模型-角色方向"和"拒绝方向"，然后对两个方向进行干预实验。具体包括：激活顺从角色方向、重新引入拒绝方向、以及在晚层窗口中投影掉角色方向。

Result: 顺从角色引导抑制拒绝行为——在Llama中拒绝率从97%降至2%。重新引入拒绝方向仅在晚层部分恢复拒绝能力，在早层则无效。在晚层窗口投影掉角色方向可恢复基线拒绝率，而投影掉随机方向则无效。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为在晚层表达阶段被门控，是在其被计算的早期阶段的下游。传统的将拒绝行为视为单一孤立方向的看法未能捕捉其对角色特征的依赖性。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [79] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlgoEvolve是一个基于LLM的进化框架,用于算法交易策略的自动生成和改进。该系统通过双层进化架构(内层程序合成+外层提示进化)在嘈杂、非平稳、高度不连续的交易环境中展现出自主的制度适应能力,始终优于人类设计的初始指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM语义变异操作符从静态编码基准扩展到更具挑战性的算法交易领域。该领域具有噪音大、非平稳性和高度不连续性的特点,是测试持续程序合成的理想场景。

Method: AlgoEvolve采用双层进化架构:内层循环使用LLM作为语义变异算子生成和迭代改进Python交易策略代码;外层循环通过元进化优化指导程序合成的提示词,发现改进的搜索启发式方法。外层进化平衡探索与利用,减少零交易失败。策略通过严格的测试协议评估。

Result: 系统展现出emergent的制度适应策略逻辑,包括交易规则的自主转变。外层循环发现的搜索启发式方法能够平衡探索与利用,显著减少零交易失败,并始终优于人类设计的初始指令。

Conclusion: LLM-based语义进化为复杂环境中的持续程序合成提供了一种可行方法。该框架在高度动态和不确定的金融环境中验证了双层进化架构的有效性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [80] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究团队开发了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合Reddit帖子、WebMD评论和FDA不良事件报告系统数据，用于分析9种抗抑郁药的安全性信息。该框架使用LLM实体识别技术，并通过Neo4j知识图谱保留溯源信息，实现了对药物不良事件的早期监测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者越来越多地在线搜索药物信息，但安全性知识分散在监管机构的不良事件记录（权威但抽象）和患者叙述（贴近体验但未经验证）之间。在精神病学领域，信息情境化不当会加剧恐惧、反安慰剂效应和治疗不依从性。将不同来源整合而不混淆证据与轶事至关重要。

Method: 开发了一个溯源感知的、基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，统一处理466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论和20年美国FDA不良事件报告系统记录，覆盖9种抗抑郁药。采用LLM实体识别管道，以医师注释为基准进行评估。基于ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA词汇表构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留每条声明的溯源信息，区分监管事实与患者体验。

Result: LLM实体识别在药物识别上达到最高F1分数0.969，在疾病识别上达到0.973。两大社区平台之间的一致性（Jaccard相似度最高达0.905）远高于它们与监管报告的一致性，表明患者生成的数据构成独立的安全信号。对于舍曲林，许多不良事件在FDA报告日期前数百天就出现在社区来源中。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了溯源感知整合是实现更可审计的精神科药物信息的途径，患者获益有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [81] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DD-Elo是一种结合漂移扩散模型(DDM)的新型国际象棋评分系统，通过整合每步棋数据来捕捉快速技能变化，比传统Elo更快适应技能变化，同时保持理论上的有界偏差和向后兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在响应滞后问题，忽略了游戏质量的细微差别。将逐棋信息纳入评分调整面临挑战，因为数据噪音大且游戏状态空间庞大。

Method: 将技能表达建模为决策过程，整合漂移扩散模型(DDM)——一种认知神经科学中的模型。通过在每步棋层面整合数据来捕捉快速技能波动。

Result: 实验证明DD-Elo比传统Elo更快适应技能变化。数学推导证实DD-Elo与传统Elo保持有界偏差。

Conclusion: DD-Elo提供了一种可解释、高响应、向后兼容的解决方案，适用于国际象棋评分生态系统。代码已开源。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [82] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于制度模式的计算治理模型，通过要求独立认证证据来约束AI智能体执行高风险不可逆行为，而非监控其推理过程。该模型保留智能体的规划和推理自主权，但剥离其对高风险操作的执行权限，并通过加密绑定意图声明、多源独立认证、确定性策略评估和防篡改日志等技术手段实现治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体正被部署于临床处方、生产环境软件部署等具有不可逆后果的高风险场景。现有治理方案主要聚焦于监控智能体的推理过程，但人类制度在治理其他自主行为体（如法人、持证专业人员）时，采用的是完全不同的范式：在关键行动节点要求提供独立认证的证据，而非追踪内部决策逻辑。这一观察揭示了当前AI治理方法与成熟人类制度实践之间的根本性差异。

Method: 研究采用制度类比与形式化方法，将人类社会的治理模式转化为计算治理模型。核心设计包括：(1) 智能体保留规划和推理的完全自主权；(2) 对指定高风险操作不持有执行权限；(3) 执行前置条件须由独立权威来源分别认证；(4) 认证信息经加密绑定至声明意图；(5) 由确定性策略进行评估；(6) 决策记录于支持独立再验证的防篡改日志。

Result: 论文完成了两项关键贡献：一是形式化提出了基于独立认证的计算治理模型框架；二是构建了概念验证实现系统，并通过软件部署和临床处方两个典型场景验证了模型的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文所提模型代表了一种从"监控推理"到"认证行动"的治理范式转变。该方法将成熟的人类制度实践（独立认证、意图绑定、可验证记录）转化为可计算执行的治理机制，为自主AI智能体的高风险操作提供了制度层面的合规保障基础，同时回避了对智能体内部推理机制进行复杂解释的难题。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [83] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI编码智能体中验证面临的根本挑战，指出传统“验证比生成容易”的直觉已被颠覆。研究从可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性三个维度评估验证信号质量，通过四类验证器（测试验证器、评分卡验证器、用户验证器、自动智能体验证器）进行实验，证明了针对性验证设计可有效抑制奖励黑客、提升任务完成质量，并得出核心结论：随着策略能力增长，验证必须与生成器共同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为验证比生成更容易，但对于现代AI编码智能体，这一直觉已被颠覆。生成复杂解决方案不再困难，但可靠地验证它们反而成为更难的问题。核心挑战在于：(1) 所有验证器都只是人类意图的代理而非意图本身，导致意图本质上难以忠实检查；(2) 模型训练过程中，优化会扩大代理与意图之间的差距，表现为奖励黑客或信号饱和。

Method: 论文从三个维度表征验证信号质量：可扩展性（scalability）、忠实性（faithfulness）和鲁棒性（robustness），并论证同时实现三者是核心挑战。研究采用四类奖励构建方法进行实验：(1) 测试验证器用于通用编码任务；(2) 评分卡验证器用于前端任务；(3) 用户作为真实世界智能体任务的验证器；(4) 自动智能体验证器用于长时域任务。

Result: 在不同任务类型和策略能力水平下进行深入分析和实验，结果表明：针对性的验证设计可有效抑制奖励黑客、改善任务完成质量，并在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著提升。实验覆盖多种场景验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 核心发现是：随着策略能力持续增长，没有任何固定的奖励函数能够保持有效；验证必须与生成器共同演化（co-evolve）。这一观察表明，静态的验证机制无法适应能力不断提升的AI系统，需要动态调整的验证策略来匹配生成器的进化。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [84] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了能源领域首个工具增强型LLM智能体基准测试，包含243个专家策划的问题，涵盖市场数据检索、知识检索和高级定量建模三大类别，评估了闭源和开源LLM在实时电力市场分析任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体基准测试已扩展至金融、编程、法律和药物发现等领域，但能源领域的评估仍局限于静态知识召回。能源行业需要实时数据检索、专业监管与市场知识以及真实约束下的多步定量推理，现有评估体系存在关键空白。

Method: 研究构建了包含243个专家策划问题的评估环境，分为三类：(1)市场数据检索与分析；(2)知识检索与解释；(3)高级定量建模与决策分析。智能体配备了可配置的领域工具套件，包括美国主要ISO的实时电力市场API、监管档案检索、公用事业费率数据库、资产优化模型和基于能源文档的检索增强生成。采用多维度评估协议，从方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐和来源有效性四个维度进行评分，并结合类别感知路由匹配评分标准。

Result: 研究提供了闭源和开源LLM的对比分析，揭示了模型能力与领域工具在高压专业领域中的交互作用。关键成果包括公开发布的评估数据和工具，以支持可复现性和未来研究。

Conclusion: 研究填补了能源领域AI智能体评估的空白，建立了涵盖实时市场数据检索、定量分析和决策支持的综合基准。公开释放的核心成果将为能源AI应用的开发评估提供基础设施支持。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [85] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym是一个统一的开源量化金融智能体开发环境，解决了现有评估平台任务碎片化、金融相关性不足的问题。该平台整合了预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等任务，提供自动化任务构建管道、容器化运行时环境防止数据泄露、低延迟交易引擎、延迟结算支持和后训练集成等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型智能体在量化金融领域的评估存在碎片化问题，仅关注孤立任务而忽视金融相关性。金融工作流程本质上是多阶段的（涵盖预测、策略构建、风险管理、交易等相互依赖的任务），现有平台仅聚焦单一任务，这会高估智能体能力，无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融决策方面的弱点。

Method: 提出OpenFinGym统一环境，集成四大模块：(1) 覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测的统一执行和验证接口；(2) 自动化任务构建管道，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(3) 容器化运行时配合主机端验证服务，支持可扩展的智能体部署并防止运行时训练-测试数据泄露；(4) 低延迟数据流设计的模拟交易引擎；(5) 支持长期预测和事件市场预测的延迟结算机制；(6) 监督微调(SFT)和强化学习(RL)后训练集成。

Result: 

Conclusion: OpenFinGym为量化金融智能体提供了全面、一致的评估和开发框架，填补了多阶段金融工作流程智能体评估的空白。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [86] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了提示词组合智能体系统中的一个新失败模式——组合行为泄漏(CBL)，即编辑一个提示模块会在没有共享变量或依赖的情况下改变其他模块的行为。实验证明仅内容通道产生可检测效应(Cohen's d=0.63)，且在标准QA中不可见但会累积影响智能体决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者报告在编辑提示词系统的某个模块时，会意外影响其他无关模块的行为。这种跨模块干扰在现有研究中缺乏正式定义和测量方法，需要建立可复用的评估协议来识别和量化这一现象。

Method: 使用三通道扰动协议(音量、内容、形式)，在Claude Sonnet 4.6上的144次试验中测试已部署的求职评估智能体。通过成对效应分析评估非焦点模块的干扰程度。

Result: 仅内容通道产生可检测的成对效应(Cohen's d=0.63, bootstrap 95% CI排除零)；无建议反转——这是一个标准QA无法检测到的亚阈值状态，但在智能体数千次决策中会累积影响。

Conclusion: CBL与已知智能体失败模式(对抗注入、认知退化、多智能体故障传播、隐私泄漏)正交。论文贡献包括：操作化定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统类别特征，将跨模块干扰测量确立为提示词组合智能体评估的必要条件。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [87] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了Kurzweil的加速回报理论，指出即使技术进步呈指数增长，这也不能解决科学发现的核心问题，因为真正的发现依赖于质性推理能力而非执行能力。文章将定性科学引擎(QES)定位为弥补这一缺失的工具，其价值不在于AGI何时到来，而在于科学发现过程本身作为值得保存和传承的人类智慧形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kurzweil的加速回报理论认为技术进步是自我强化的指数增长，但作者认为这种加速并不能解决科学发现的核心问题——即判断现有框架何时在结构上不足，以及需要什么概念性突破。ARC-AGI-3测试显示人类达到上限而前沿AI系统仍低于1%，表明当前AI与人类灵活推理之间存在巨大差距。

Method: 首先对Kurzweil理论给出简单的数学解释，然后区分两种能力：执行性/基础设施能力（适合加速发展）和质性推理能力（真正的科学发现所依赖的）。引用ARC-AGI-3基准测试结果和Hassabis的观点来支撑论点。

Result: 加速回报理论主要适用于执行性和基础设施能力，而科学发现更依赖于质性推理。QES被定位为解决这一缺失能力的方案，其价值独立于AGI实现时间表。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于：(1)澄清了加速回报理论的适用范围局限性；(2)将QES定位为应对科学发现中质性推理需求的工具；(3)强调科学发现过程本身就是一种值得保存和传承的人类智慧形式。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [88] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Narration-of-Thought (NoT)方法，通过结构化思维链提示（包含主人公、利益相关者、双步后果、不确定性、承诺五个部分）解决道德推理中的利益相关者崩溃和不确定性抑制两大失败模式。该方法无需训练或微调，在100个DailyDilemmas场景中将利益相关者崩溃从31%降至1%以下，不确定性抑制从72%降至1-24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链推理在道德困境中存在两个关键失败模式：(1)利益相关者崩溃——推理过程仅命名最多一个利益相关方；(2)不确定性抑制——在承诺行动前缺乏明确的未知因素或保留表述。这两个问题阻碍了AI在需要可靠决策的场景中的部署。

Method: 提出NoT系统提示，将思维链结构化为五个部分：主人公识别→利益相关者列举→双步后果分析→不确定性表达→行动承诺。采用文本梯度下降优化支架，跨家族训练评判模型（使用与生成模型不同的供应商）进行评估。扩展为五轮多方辩论协议以达成共识。

Result: NoT将利益相关者崩溃率从最高31%降至1%以下，不确定性抑制从72%降至1-24%。利益相关者数量Cliff's delta优势达+0.79至+0.90，不确定性评分优势+0.65至+0.93。共识达成率从6%提升至95%（校准集）和100%（DailyDilemmas复制集）。

Conclusion: NoT通过结构化提示有效改善道德推理质量，无需额外训练成本。扩展的多方辩论协议显著提升共识达成率。产生的推理痕迹外部化了决策依据，为可靠的自主智能体部署提供了可审计的基础。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [89] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出几何感知蒙特卡洛树搜索(G-A MCTS)框架来解决组合几何中的极值问题，通过增量约束更新和对称性利用，在多个经典问题上取得了新的最优计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合几何极值问题存在组合爆炸问题，经典精确求解器难以扩展；标准强化学习和Transformer模型受限于稀疏奖励的「有效性悬崖」和二次方 token 消耗限制，无法有效解决 n×n 网格上的严格全局几何约束配置问题。

Method: 提出几何感知MCTS框架，通过两种关键技术突破瓶颈：(1) 增量更新可行动作空间来严格强制几何约束，将共线性检查复杂度从 O(n³) 降至 O(n²)；(2) 利用几何对称性——在节点扩展时进行规范剪枝以降低分支因子，以及对称批量转换加速发现有效配置。

Result: 在6个问题中的5个上建立了新的最佳已知计算结果：Max-N3IL 问题在 82≤n≤119 的网格上找到约 1.8n 大小的配置；Smallest Complete Set 问题在测试网格上找到约 0.95n 大小的配置，提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: 几何感知MCTS是一个高度适应性框架，可用于发现组合几何中的新配置。核心贡献在于将几何约束信息集成到搜索过程中，通过复杂度降低和对称性利用显著提升了大规模问题的求解效率。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [90] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种智能聚合器框架,用于协调电动公交车队与电网的交互,通过将优化调度模型与监督智能体结合,实现对充电和V2G灵活性的自适应协调。但研究表明,profit-oriented模式下智能体可能从公共交通运营商处提取价值,部署时需透明的协调模式和明确的价值分配规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队运营需要持续协调服务可靠性、电池荷电状态、充电器可用性、电价、路线能耗不确定性和车辆到电网(V2G)机会等多重因素,现有方法难以有效处理这种复杂的动态决策环境。

Method: 提出智能聚合器框架,将基于优化的电动公交调度模型与监督智能体相结合。优化核心在路线、充电器、电池和V2G交换方面强制物理可行性;智能体层解释变化的运营条件,在需要时触发实时重新优化,并定义灵活性价值在聚合器与公共交通运营商(PTO)之间的分配方式。

Result: 使用真实 depot 案例评估日间前和实时运营,涵盖基于利润和基于运营的协调模式,考虑服务延迟、路线能耗偏差、电价冲击和组合干扰。结果表明:智能聚合可维持可行调度、选择性激活重新优化、改善充电和V2G灵活性利用。

Conclusion: 智能聚合器可支持自适应车队-电网协调,但存在关键权衡:同样的智能体能力在profit-oriented定价配置下可能从PTO提取价值。部署于公共车队场景需透明的协调模式、可审计的 tariff 设置和明确的价值分配规则。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [91] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究预言机AI的自我指涉问题，提出使用闭置信集（credal set）的最小不动点作为规范答案，既无偏又自洽，且在非表现性问题中退化为经典点估计，二元事件下得到区间形式答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预言机AI在报告未来事件概率时存在自我指涉困境：当答案被学习并据此行动时，会改变原本报告的概率。现有方案（如反事实问题）虽能规避，但答案在得知瞬间即失效，变得无关紧要。

Method: 1) 将答案从单一概率扩展为置信集；2) 要求答案对"被学习后会产生的影响"无偏且自洽；3) 利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理，在闭置信集完备格上定义单调算子，取其最小不动点；4) 变体方法取包含所有自洽点估计的最小不动点。

Result: 1) 证明了最小不动点的存在性、自洽性和非空性；2) 证明了对于非表现性问题，该构造退化为经典点估计；3) 在二元事件下，规范答案是闭区间形式（需自然的外壳分解假设）；4) 整个框架可从二元事件B推广到任意随机变量X，条件概率P(B|A,C)替换为条件分布L(X|A,C)。

Conclusion: 通过纯格论方法建立了自洽预言机的数学基础，但开放问题仍存在：1) 区间特征是否在一般随机变量情形下保持；2) 多个自洽不动点的取舍标准；3) 该框架与现有决策理论的衔接。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [92] [Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26454)
*Tiansi Dong,Mateja Jamnik,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明监督式深度学习无法达到符号级三段论推理水平，主要原因包括训练数据无法区分24种有效三段论类型，以及端到端映射在模式识别和逻辑推理之间引入矛盾的训练目标。实验表明Euler Net无法实现严格的三段论推理，ChatGPT GPT-5可能达到100%准确率但提供错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球形神经网络已实现符号级三段论推理，但数据驱动的机器学习系统是否能通过增加训练数据和时间达到同等水平仍是未解决的科学问题。

Method: 从方法论角度分析监督深度学习的两个根本局限：(1)分析训练数据能否区分24种有效三段论类型；(2)分析端到端映射是否在模式识别和逻辑推理的神经组件间引入矛盾训练目标。同时进行实验验证，包括Euler Net测试和对ChatGPT(GPT-5-nano和GPT-5)在四种表面形式下的三段论语句可满足性判断测试。

Result: 理论分析证明监督深度学习存在两个方法论局限：(1)训练数据无法区分24种三段论类型；(2)端到端映射引入矛盾训练目标。Euler Net无法实现严格的三段论推理。ChatGPT测试显示：表面形式影响推理性能，GPT-5可能达到100%准确率但提供错误解释。

Conclusion: 监督式机器学习系统无法达到符号逻辑推理的严格性，即使训练过程在达到100%准确率后停止，也无法获得真正的逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: Sphere neural networks have achieved symbolic level syllogistic reasoning without training data, raising the question of where the limit of the scaling law for logical reasoning lies, i.e., whether data-driven machine learning systems can achieve the same level by increasing training data and training time. We show two methodological limitations that prevent supervised deep learning from reaching the symbolic-level syllogistic reasoning: (1) training data can not distinguish all 24 types of valid syllogistic reasoning; (2) end-to-end mapping from premises to conclusion introduces contradictory training targets between neural components for pattern recognition and logical reasoning. Beside theoretical analysis, we experimentally illustrate that Euler Net cannot achieve rigorous syllogistic reasoning. We further challenge the most recent ChatGPTs (GPT-5-nano and GPT-5) to determine the satisfiability of syllogistic statements in four surface forms (patterns): words, double words, simple symbols, and long random symbols, showing that surface forms affect the reasoning performance and that ChatGPT GPT-5 may reach 100% accuracy but still provide incorrect explanations. As empirical training processes are stopped after achieving 100% accuracy, we conclude that supervised machine learning systems will not attain the rigour of symbolic logical reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [MKG-RAG-Bench: Benchmarking Retrieval in Multimodal Knowledge Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26458)
*Xiaochen Wang,Bao Hoang,Han Liu,Ting Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MKG-RAG-Bench，一个专门用于评估多模态知识图谱检索增强生成(MKG-RAG)中检索组件的跨领域基准测试。该基准覆盖通用和医学领域，通过LLM策展流程构建，包含对齐的问答数据集和多样化的模态配置。实验表明有效的多模态检索仍是挑战，但对端到端性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG基准测试主要针对非结构化文本或多模态语料库，忽视了多模态知识图谱场景中检索的特殊挑战，包括跨模态异构性、对齐困难等问题。

Method: 构建了跨通用和医学领域的两个多模态知识图谱；设计LLM驱动的策展管道，过滤低效用知识，生成结构化查询并提供精确监督；系统覆盖多种模态配置组合；包含精心对齐的问答数据集。

Result: 实验覆盖代表性检索器家族和多种模态设置，结果表明有效多模态检索仍然困难但对端到端性能至关重要，检索质量强烈决定生成效果。

Conclusion: MKG-RAG-Bench通过将检索作为一等评估目标，为诊断当前局限性提供了原则性基础，并为推动多模态知识图谱RAG系统的进步提供支撑。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs has emerged as a promising approach for grounding large language models, yet existing benchmarks largely overlook the challenges of retrieval in multimodal knowledge graph RAG (MKG-RAG). In practice, retrieval is a critical bottleneck: multimodal knowledge is heterogeneous, difficult to align across modalities, and often poorly served by retrievers designed for unstructured corpora. To address this gap, we introduce MKG-RAG-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate retrieval in MKG-RAG. MKG-RAG-Bench is constructed from two multimodal knowledge graphs spanning general and medical domains, and includes carefully aligned question-answering datasets that support controlled evaluation of both retrieval and downstream generation. The benchmark is built using an LLM-based curation pipeline that filters low-utility knowledge, generates structurally grounded queries with exact supervision, and systematically covers diverse modality configurations. Through extensive experiments across representative retriever families and modality settings, we show that effective multimodal retrieval remains challenging yet crucial for end-to-end MKG-RAG performance, and that retrieval quality strongly determines generation outcomes. By isolating retrieval as a first-class evaluation target, MKG-RAG-Bench provides a principled foundation for diagnosing current limitations and advancing multimodal knowledge graph RAG systems.

</details>


### [94] [auto-psych: Automating the science of mind using agent-driven theory discovery and experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26460)
*Ben Prystawski,Kushin Mukherjee,Daniel Wurgaft,Linas Nasvytis,Michael Y. Li,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了auto-psych系统，一个用于计算认知科学的自动化理论发现与数据收集框架。该系统通过嵌套的智能体架构，在众包平台上自动设计实验、收集人类被试数据并生成解释性认知模型。测试用例为经典的硬币翻转序列随机性判断任务，系统成功从综合数据和人类实验中恢复了优于文献的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现流程中的数据收集是瓶颈。心理学和计算认知科学具有独特优势：理论常以代码形式表示，众包平台可实现大规模程序化数据收集。该研究旨在实现自动化假设生成、实验设计和数据分析的完整流程。

Method: auto-psych系统采用嵌套智能体发现循环：内层循环进行认知模型的猜想、拟合和批判；外层循环设计实验、在在线平台启动实验并分析数据。使用硬币翻转序列随机性判断作为经典测试场景。

Result: 系统能从综合数据中通过系统性实验快速可靠地恢复真实理论，且嵌套结构对模型性能至关重要。在三组独立的人类实验中，系统生成的模型拟合效果优于来自科学文献的理论。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在计算认知科学中进行自动化数据收集和理论发现的可行性，为AI驱动的研究自动化提供了实证基础。

Abstract: AI-based scientific automation is increasingly possible by using agents to generate hypotheses, design experiments, and analyze data. Data collection is a major bottleneck in this pipeline, however. Psychology, and computational cognitive science in particular, is well-positioned to benefit from AI experimentation because theories are often represented as code and crowdsourcing platforms enable programmatic human data collection at scale. Here, we apply automated discovery techniques to the project of generating theories in computational cognitive science, with an agent-based system collecting human data independently through crowdsourced survey experiments. As a testbed, we use a classic case study from cognitive psychology: judging which sequences of coin flips seem subjectively more random. Our system, auto-psych, uses nested agent-based discovery loops to generate explanatory theories of human behavior. The inner loop conjectures, fits, and critiques probabilistic cognitive models; the outer loop designs experiments to test these models, launches them online, and analyzes the data. This system can quickly and reliably recover ground-truth theories from synthetic data via systematic experimentation, but the nested structure is critical to model performance. Further, in three independent sequences of human experiments, the system finds theories that fit the data better than theories generated from the scientific literature. This work thus demonstrates the feasibility of automated data collection and theory discovery in computational cognitive science.

</details>


### [95] [Clinical Harness for Governable Medical AI Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26494)
*Tianhan Xu,Lei Bao,Yongxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了临床AI技能(Clinical AI Skills)和Clinical Harness运行时治理架构，旨在解决医疗AI在临床应用中缺乏持久性和可问责性的问题，以骨质疏松症为例展示了知识驱动、数据驱动和物理增强技能的整合应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学AI以孤立模型形式存在，缺乏跨时间维度的持续性和可问责能力，无法满足临床护理对AI系统的实际需求。临床环境需要AI具备可追溯、可监控、可管理的特性，而非一次性模型调用。

Method: 提出Clinical Harness作为运行时治理架构，具备四大核心功能：注册(registering)、编排(orchestrating)、防护(guarding)和监控(monitoring)AI支持的临床能力。研究以骨质疏松症作为示范场景，展示三类技能的整合：知识驱动技能、数据驱动技能和物理增强技能。

Result: 通过骨质疏松症案例验证了所提架构的可行性，展示了如何支持生命周期护理和运行时治理。研究提供了将孤立AI模型转化为可问责、可管理临床能力的系统性方法。

Conclusion: Clinical Harness为医学AI从孤立模型向持续性临床能力转变提供了架构基础，有望推动AI在临床环境中的规范化、可信部署。

Abstract: Medical AI remains organized around isolated models, whereas clinical care requires accountable capabilities that persist across time. We propose clinical AI skills and the Clinical Harness: a runtime governance architecture for registering, orchestrating, guarding and monitoring AI-enabled clinical capabilities. Using osteoporosis as an exemplar, we show how knowledge-driven, data-driven and physics-enhanced skills can support lifecycle care under runtime governance.

</details>


### [96] [Humans Disengage, Reasoning Models Persist: Separating Difficulty Registration from Deliberation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26502)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现大型推理模型(LRMs)和人类在困难问题上都花费更多时间/token，表现出表面相似性，但在固定题目后出现反向模式：LRMs答错时token更多，人类答错时时间更少。通过「注册」与「分配」的分离分析，揭示两者共享难度信号但实现相反的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前研究观察到LRMs和人类在困难问题上都花更多时间，得出两者有相似的「思考」机制。但该研究指出这种表面相似性掩盖了题目内(item)的反向模式，需要更细致的分析框架来揭示两者的根本差异。

Method: 使用人类-LRM匹配语料库，通过「注册(registration)」和「分配(allocation)」两层 deliberation 分离进行分析：(1)注册：追踪响应时间如何随题目难度变化；(2)分配：在题目身份固定下，比较同一题目的成功与失败尝试。比较了5种thinking LRMs，使用Cohen's d量化效应量，应用题目固定效应控制变量。

Result: 所有5个LRMs在H-ARC数据集上显示出大的wrong-vs-right效应(Cohen's d=1.47-3.13)，方向与人类相反。非thinking基线模型不显示此分离。在难度校准上两者一致，但在题目内比较时出现系统性分歧。

Conclusion: 人类模式代表「engagement vs abandonment」(坚持vs放弃)：预期能解决的题继续做，预计失败的题放弃。LRM模式由「不确定性驱动长度」(length driven by uncertainty)：模型不确定时chain变长，而这恰恰是它容易失败的时候。两种策略产生相同的跨题目-难度相关性，但只有题目内分析才能揭示差异。资源理性元推理视角下，两者是共享难度信号但实现相反控制的两种停止策略。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) take longer on harder problems, just as humans do. This surface similarity hides an opposite pattern within items. When an LRM gets a problem wrong, it spends more tokens than when it gets the same problem right; humans do the reverse, spending less time on the trials they get wrong. We separate two levels of deliberation: how response time tracks difficulty across items (registration), and, with item identity held fixed, whether an agent spends more on its own failures or successes (allocation). On a public matched human-LRM corpus, humans and all five thinking LRMs reproduce the known cross-item alignment (registration) but diverge within items (allocation): every LRM shows a large wrong-vs-right effect (Cohen's d = 1.47-3.13 on H-ARC) while humans show the opposite sign. The comparison stays inside each agent's own scale; we never put seconds and tokens on one axis. The dissociation holds under item fixed effects, replicates across datasets, and is absent in a non-thinking baseline. We read the human pattern as engagement versus abandonment: people stay on items they expect to solve and give up on the rest. We read the LRM pattern as length driven by uncertainty: chains grow when the model is unsure, which is exactly when it tends to fail. Both policies produce the same cross-item correlation with difficulty, so they look aligned on the measure prior work has used; the divergence shows up only once item identity is fixed. Under resource-rational metareasoning, the split is between two stopping policies that share a difficulty signal but implement opposite control; trace length captures the signal and misses the control.

</details>


### [97] [NeuraDock Visual Cognitive Load Agent Tutorial: A Quality-Gated Open-Source EEG Workflow for Alpha Dynamics and Real-Time Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26518)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Yueqing Dai,Junling Li,Junwen Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程性论文，介绍了开源EEG智能体NeuraDock Agent，专注于Alpha动态和视觉认知负荷分析，旨在为研究者提供从EEG数据到实时视觉认知负荷原型的完整、透明的技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG工具包虽提供优秀的离线分析功能，但构建实时、质量门控的认知负荷处理流程需要手动整合采集、自定义QC、Alpha特征提取和Web API等多个组件，存在显著的离线到在线gap。

Method: 采用质量门控工作流程：仅在预处理和QC门控后才计算下游Alpha和工作负荷指标。提供完整分步教程涵盖：安装部署、EEG预处理与质量控制、Alpha动态可视化、组内Rest/Task视觉认知负荷比较、公开迷你数据集分析、在线仪表板启动、外部应用实时API调用及LLM解释层质量风险评估。

Result: 迷你数据集验证中，智能体处理了18个 recordings，生成10个组内比较，其中7个显示出任务相关的后部Alpha抑制，初步证明了组内可重复性，并基准测试了本地在线API延迟性能。

Conclusion: 教程为研究者、开发者和应用团队提供了从EEG文件到实时视觉认知负荷原型开发的具体、透明的技术路径，有效弥合了离线分析与在线部署之间的技术鸿沟。

Abstract: This tutorial paper provides a step-by-step, reproducible walkthrough of NeuraDock Agent, an open-source EEG agent focused on Alpha dynamics and visual cognitive-load analysis. The goal is practical: a reader should be able to install the agent, run EEG preprocessing and quality control, generate Alpha dynamics figures, perform within-subject Rest/Task visual cognitive-load comparison, run the public mini-dataset analyses and compare them with the reference validation summary, start an online dashboard, call the real-time API from an external application, and use the LLM interpretation layer to explain quality risks. Existing EEG toolkits provide excellent offline analysis, but assembling a real-time, quality-gated cognitive-load pipeline often requires manually bridging acquisition, custom QC, Alpha feature extraction, and a web API; this tutorial closes that offline-to-online gap. The tutorial uses a quality-gated workflow: downstream Alpha and workload metrics are computed only after preprocessing and QC gating rather than directly from raw EEG. In the included mini-dataset validation, the agent processed 18 recordings, generated 10 within-subject comparisons, observed task-related posterior Alpha suppression in 7 of 10 contrasts, estimated initial evidence of within-subject repeatability, and benchmarked local online API latency. The tutorial is intended for researchers, developers, and applied teams who want a transparent path from EEG files to real-time visual cognitive-load prototypes.

</details>


### [98] [Boundary-Aware Context Grounding for A Low-Channel EEG Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26519)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Yueqing Dai,Junling Li,Junwen Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuraDock Agent是一个开源架构，通过将确定性本地EEG引擎与硬件感知语言层分离，使LLM能够更安全地处理低通道脑电图数据。该系统通过仅向LLM提供精简的白名单摘要和版本化上下文来约束AI行为，避免不合理的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM不自动了解特定传感器的测量能力、软件实现的算法或计算结果支持的结论，这在低通道EEG中尤为重要，因为稀疏空间覆盖和可变信号质量使得产生看似合理但实际不支持的解读变得容易。

Method: 提出NeuraDock Agent架构，将确定性本地EEG数值引擎与硬件感知语言层分离。数值引擎解析记录、执行质量控制、执行审查的光谱工作流并写入机器可读产物；LLM仅接收紧凑的白名单摘要和版本化上下文包，包含硬件描述、工作流、结果字段、实现边界和参考案例。

Result: 评估分为三个层面：(1)12个记录的十次数值重复产生相同结构化结果，完整Rest/Task运行在三次重复中产生相同结果、报告和图表哈希；(2)请求捕获和故障注入实验确认了数据边界和本地产物的保留；(3)边界感知基准测试在四种上下文消融和两种LLM下测试36个普通和对抗性问题，产生288个输出。

Conclusion: 结果表明硬件和实现感知的接地机制是校准EEG代理接受、限定或拒绝内容的实用机制，但未建立临床有效性或经过验证的绝对认知负荷指数。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can make scientific software easier to use. However, a general model does not automatically know which measurements a particular sensor can support, which algorithms are implemented in the current software, or which conclusions are justified by a computed result. These distinctions are especially important for low-channel electroencephalography (EEG), where sparse spatial coverage and variable signal quality make plausible but unsupported interpretations easy to produce. We present NeuraDock Agent, an open-source architecture that separates a deterministic local EEG engine from a hardware-aware language layer. The numerical engine parses recordings, performs quality control, executes reviewed spectral workflows, and writes machine-readable artifacts. The LLM receives only a compact, allowlisted summary and a versioned context pack. The context describes the seven-channel hardware, reviewed workflows, result fields, implementation boundaries, scientific limits, and reference cases. Raw EEG and dense per-sample arrays remain local
  We evaluate the system at three levels. First, 12 recordings produced identical structured results over ten numerical repetitions, and a complete Rest/Task run produced identical result, report, and figure hashes over three repetitions. Second, request-capture and failure-injection experiments confirmed the tested data boundary and preservation of local artifacts under HTTP, malformed-output, and connection failures. Third, a boundary-awareness benchmark tested 36 ordinary and adversarial questions under four context ablations and two LLMs, yielding 288 outputs.These results support hardware- and implementation-aware grounding as a practical mechanism for calibrating what an EEG agent accepts, qualifies, or refuses; they do not establish clinical validity or a validated absolute cognitive-load index.

</details>


### [99] [Radical AI Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26523)
*Daniel A. Herrmann,Benjamin A. Levinstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合哲学激进解释传统与机制可解释性工具的AI系统解释框架，用于从计算事实推导AI的信念、欲望和意义。框架包含表征主义和解释主义两种方法的评判标准，并与当前可解释性测试挂钩。核心论点是信念、欲望及其预设的命题结构必须联合约束，而非逐个单独认定，否则会引入扭曲。这种整体论对AI系统尤为重要，因为AI可能不具备解释者共享的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被广泛部署，需要能够信任这些系统——无论是通过理解其目标，还是更保守地通过可靠检测欺骗行为。当前可解释性研究者正在构建从模型内部读取信念和欲望的工具，但尚无统一标准来判断这类工具何时成功。

Method: 借鉴哲学激进解释传统，结合机制可解释性工具，提出对表征主义和解释主义方法的评判标准，并将每种标准与当前可解释性方法可执行的测试相联系。

Result: 核心发现：信念、欲望及其预设的命题结构必须联合约束，而非逐个固定后再测量；AI系统可能不具备解释者共享的概念，这使整体论方法变得紧迫；但同时这种整体论也提供了解释杠杆。

Conclusion: AI系统的态度约束其命题结构，该结构又约束可归因的态度类型，机制可解释性可以帮助测量这两者，从而建立可靠的解释框架。

Abstract: We develop a framework for interpreting AI systems as agents, drawing on the philosophical tradition of radical interpretation and the tools of mechanistic interpretability. The core question is: given the computational facts about a system, how do we solve for its beliefs, desires, and meanings? This matters increasingly for safety. We want to be able to trust the systems we deploy, whether by understanding their goals or, more modestly, by reliably detecting deception. Interpretability researchers are building tools to read beliefs and desires off a model's internals, but there is no settled account of when such a tool has succeeded. This book supplies one. We propose criteria on both representationalist and interpretationist approaches, and tie each to tests current interpretability methods can carry out. A central lesson is that these attributions cannot be made piecemeal. Beliefs, desires, and the propositional structure they presuppose are jointly constrained, and a method that fixes one while measuring the others inherits whatever distortions that introduces. This holism becomes pressing for AI systems, which may not share the interpreter's concepts. However, it also provides leverage: a system's attitudes constrain its propositional structure, that structure constrains which attitudes can be attributed, and mechanistic interpretability can help us measure both.

</details>


### [100] [PMDformer: Patch-Mean Decoupling Information Transformer for Long-term Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26549)
*Ao Hu,Liangjian Wen,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,He Yan,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Yukun Zhang,Ruoxi Jiang,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PMDformer通过补丁均值解耦(PMD)、趋势恢复注意力(TRA)和近端变量注意力(PVA)三个模块，有效解决Transformer模型在长期时间序列预测中因尺度差异导致形状相似性捕捉困难的问题，在多个LTSF基准上实现了稳定性和准确性的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的模型在长期时间序列预测中采用补丁策略捕获长程依赖，但由于不同补丁和变量间的尺度差异，难以准确建模形状相似性，这限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出PMDformer框架，包含三个核心组件：1) PMD通过减去每个补丁均值分离趋势与残差形状信息；2) TRA在计算注意力时重新整合解耦的趋势；3) PVA聚焦于最相关的最近时间段进行跨变量注意力计算，避免对过时相关性的过拟合。

Result: 实验表明PMDformer在多个长期时间序列预测基准上优于现有最先进方法，在稳定性和准确性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: PMDformer通过有效分离和重建趋势信息，并结合近端变量注意力机制，成功提升了长程预测的形状相似性捕获能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) plays a crucial role in fields such as energy management, finance, and traffic prediction. Transformer-based models have adopted patch-based strategies to capture long-range dependencies, but accurately modeling shape similarities across patches and variables remains challenging due to scale differences. To address this, we introduce patch-mean decoupling (PMD), which separates the trend and residual shape information by subtracting the mean of each patch, preserving the original structure and ensuring that the attention mechanism captures true shape similarities. Futhermore, to more effectively model long-range dependencies and capture cross-variable relationships, we propose Trend Restoration Attention (TRA) and Proximal Variable Attention (PVA). The former module reintegrates the decoupled trend from PMD while calculating attention output. And the latter focuses cross-variable attention on the most relevant, recent time segments to avoid overfitting on outdated correlations. Combining these components, we propose PMDformer, a model designed to effectively capture shape similarity in long-term forecasting scenarios. Extensive experiments indicate that PMDformer outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in stability and accuracy across multiple LTSF benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/aohu1105/PMDformer.

</details>


### [101] [Explainable Ensemble-Based Machine Learning Models for Detecting the Presence of Cirrhosis in Hepatitis C Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26561)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Lujain Alnajrani,Nawal Alsheikh,Alhatoon Alanazy,Salam Alshammasi,Meshael Almusairii,Shoog Alrassan,Aisha Alansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究利用机器学习算法（随机森林、梯度提升机、XGBoost和Extra Trees）诊断丙型肝炎患者的肝硬化。Extra Trees模型表现最佳，准确率达96.92%，召回率94.00%，精确率99.81%，AUC为96%，且仅使用16个特征。该研究首次将机器学习应用于丙型肝炎相关肝硬化的早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丙型肝炎病毒感染可导致肝脏炎症，长期可发展为肝硬化甚至肝衰竭。由于患者在发病前可能数十年无明显症状，肝硬化的早期诊断对于防止并发症至关重要。然而，目前尚未有研究将机器学习技术应用于丙型肝炎患者肝硬化的检测。

Method: 研究使用UCI机器学习库中来自埃及的2038例患者数据集，包含28个属性。采用四种机器学习算法进行训练和比较：随机森林(Random Forest)、梯度提升机(Gradient Boosting Machine)、极限梯度提升(XGBoost)和极端随机树(Extra Trees)模型。

Result: Extra Trees模型在所有评估指标上均优于其他模型：准确率96.92%、召回率94.00%、精确率99.81%、AUC 96%。该模型仅使用28个特征中的16个特征即达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法，特别是Extra Trees模型，能够有效识别丙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者，性能优异且具有特征筛选能力。该研究为肝硬化的早期无创诊断提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Hepatitis C is a liver infection caused by a virus, which results in mild to severe inflammation of the liver. Over many years, hepatitis C gradually damages the liver, often leading to permanent scarring, known as cirrhosis. Patients sometimes have moderate or no symptoms of liver illness for decades before developing cirrhosis. Cirrhosis typically worsens to the point of liver failure. Patients with cirrhosis may also experience brain and nerve system damage, as well as gastrointestinal hemorrhage. Treatment for cirrhosis focuses on preventing further progression of the disease. Detecting cirrhosis earlier is therefore crucial for avoiding complications. Machine learning (ML) has been shown to be effective at providing precise and accurate information for use in diagnosing several diseases. Despite this, no studies have so far used ML to detect cirrhosis in patients with hepatitis C. This study obtained a dataset consisting of 28 attributes of 2038 Egyptian patients from the ML Repository of the University of California at Irvine. Four ML algorithms were trained on the dataset to diagnose cirrhosis in hepatitis C patients: a Random Forest, a Gradient Boosting Machine, an Extreme Gradient Boosting, and an Extra Trees model. The Extra Trees model outperformed the other models achieving an accuracy of 96.92%, a recall of 94.00%, a precision of 99.81%, and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 96% using only 16 of the 28 features.

</details>


### [102] [EvoOptiGraph: Weakness-Driven Coevolution via Graph-Based Structural Generation for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26578)
*Qingcan Kang,Mingyang Liu,Xiaojin Fu,Shixiong Kai,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出EvoOptiGraph框架，通过数据和模型协同演化解决LLM自动化优化建模的两大挑战：训练语料结构多样性不足和数据生成流程与模型学习脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动化优化建模面临两个关键挑战：训练语料缺乏结构多样性，以及数据生成流程与模型学习静态解耦。

Method: EvoOptiGraph将混合整数线性规划(MILP)表示为属性二部图，应用有效性保持的进化算子生成结构多样化实例。通过确定性编译和验证回译将图转换为求解器代码和自然语言。训练采用两阶段：监督微调(SFT)后进行基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)，利用图衍生弱点信号指导针对模型失败的实例生成。

Result: 在六个公开数据集上的实验表明，EvoOptiGraph在准确性、可执行性和泛化能力上显著优于更大的通用模型、代理方法和专门基线。

Conclusion: 有针对性的数据-模型协同演化是提升LLM优化建模能力的有效策略。

Abstract: Automating optimization modeling from natural language with large language models (LLMs) faces two key challenges. First, training corpora lack structural diversity. Second, data generation pipelines remain static and decoupled from model learning. To address these challenges, we propose EvoOptiGraph, a novel framework where data and model co-evolve, driven by model weaknesses. EvoOptiGraph represents each mixed-integer linear program (MILP) as an attributed bipartite graph and applies validity-preserving evolutionary operators to generate structurally diverse instances. The evolved graphs are converted into solver code and natural language via deterministic compilation and verified back-translation. Training proceeds in two stages: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on an initial dataset, followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where graph-derived weakness signals guide the generation of new instances targeting the model's failures. This forms a closed loop that continuously updates the training distribution. Empirical results on six public datasets show that EvoOptiGraph significantly outperforms larger generalist models, agentic methods, and specialized baselines in accuracy, executability, and generalization. These results demonstrate that targeted data-model coevolution is an effective strategy for improving LLMs on optimization modeling tasks.

</details>


### [103] [A Multi-Level Validation and Traceability Framework for AI-Generated Telescope Scheduling Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26585)
*Hengchu Xiao,Chuanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多层次验证和可追溯推理框架，用于在AI生成的望远镜调度决策执行前进行系统性可靠性验证，通过数据引用验证、逻辑一致性检查和约束验证过滤无效决策，并引入原子推理单元支持错误定位。实验表明该框架显著提升了AI调度的可执行性和可靠性，为AI在天文观测调度中的高可靠性应用提供了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在望远镜调度中的应用虽然能处理复杂多约束问题，但其输出存在数据引用不一致、推理错误和不可执行决策等问题，限制了在高可靠性观测任务中的适用性。

Method: 提出了多层次验证与可追溯推理框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 数据引用验证；(2) 逻辑一致性检查；(3) 观测和仪器约束验证。框架引入原子推理单元及其依赖关系，将调度决策表示为相互关联的推理步骤序列，支持错误定位和事后分析。

Result: 实验表明该框架提高了AI调度的可执行性和可靠性，减少了瞬态机会损失。反馈校正和结构化推理验证增强了对错误决策的修复和阻断能力，特别是在复杂场景中。

Conclusion: 框架增强的方法在保持AI灵活性的同时显著提升了可靠性和可执行性，为AI应用于高可靠性天文观测调度提供了可行且可验证的路径。

Abstract: With the gradual introduction of AI into telescope scheduling, AI-based decision-making has shown advantages in handling complex multi-constraint problems. However, its outputs often suffer from inconsistent data references, reasoning errors, and non-executable decisions, limiting applicability in high-reliability observational tasks. In this work, we propose a multi-level validation and traceable reasoning framework that performs systematic reliability verification of AI-generated decisions prior to execution, and enables explicit representation of the reasoning process to support traceable decision-making. The framework integrates data reference validation, logical consistency checks, and observational and instrumental constraint verification to filter and correct invalid decisions. It also introduces atomic reasoning units and their dependency relationships, representing scheduling decisions as a sequence of interconnected reasoning steps that support error localization and post hoc analysis. Experiments show that the framework improves executability and reliability of AI scheduling and reduces loss of transient opportunities. In particular, feedback correction and structured validation of reasoning steps enhance the ability to repair and block erroneous decisions, especially in complex scenarios. Compared with pure AI methods, the framework-enhanced approach maintains flexibility while substantially improving reliability and executability. These results demonstrate a feasible and verifiable pathway for applying AI to high-reliability astronomical observation scheduling.

</details>


### [104] [Content-Based Smart E-Mail Dispatcher Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26593)
*K. Paramesha,K R Sriram,Sujan Shetty,Shamanth Kishore,R. Tejaswini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM智能体的邮件自动分发系统，通过分析邮件内容将邮件路由至相应WhatsApp群组，避免手动处理邮件的错误和时间消耗，提升组织信息流转效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着邮件通信量不断增加，大型组织在处理和转发邮件时面临效率低下、错误频发的问题。传统的人工阅读邮件并转发至即时通讯平台的方式耗时长、易出错，导致生产力损失和员工压力增大。

Method: 系统采用基于LLM智能体的框架，通过结构化提示词将邮件内容作为输入，结合指令和上下文信息，由LLM分析判断邮件应分发至哪个WhatsApp学生群组。该方法无需标注数据集，利用LLM的文本理解能力进行决策。

Result: 系统实现了邮件内容的自动分析和智能路由，将相关信息及时送达目标群组，在无需标注数据的情况下完成分发任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于LLM智能体的邮件分发系统为组织信息流转提供了高效解决方案，显著提升了工作效率并减轻了阅读邮件的认知负担，具备良好的实用价值和可扩展性。

Abstract: Email communication has become an integral part of personal and professional life, but handling its vast volume is still a significant issue for large organisations. Manual perusal of emails and forwarding their contents and attachments to intended recipients using other instant messaging platforms has proved to be error-prone and time-consuming leading to losses in terms of productivity and creating undue stress. The main objective of this paper is to explore an alternative mechanism that is to automate the task of dispatching emails based on their contents to the respective WhatsApp groups of students of various semesters of programs in an engineering college, facilitating a smooth flow of information from one end to another end in an organisation. The dispatcher system is built using agents querying large language models (LLMs) to enable it to analyze the contents of emails and route them to the relevant groups of students for their information and consumption. The system harnesses the capabilities of LLMs in analysing the textual contents for decision-making. With a well-structured agent framework prompt that includes email content as input with instructions and context, the system figures out the relevant groups to which the email message is dispatched, thus providing the required information on time. The proposed system does not rely on labelled datasets and provides several benefits, including enhanced productivity and a reduction in the cognitive load associated with reading emails.

</details>


### [105] [LLM-based Models for Detecting Emerging Topics in Service Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26595)
*Mahsa Tavakoli,Ruth Bankey,Cristián Bravo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型(LLM)、统计技术与人机协作的多语言客户服务反馈分析框架,用于税务管理等公共部门识别服务质量问题和潜在服务不平等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务反馈分析依赖人工审核或静态指标,难以扩展且无法捕捉文本反馈中的复杂模式。随着反馈量增长,公共部门(尤其是税务机构)亟需自动化工具来识别新兴服务质量问题和不同人群间的潜在服务差异。

Method: 框架结合微调、量化后的LLM与专家监督,通过相似度分析和税务官员评估进行验证,并引入人在环(human-in-the-loop)机制以减少LLM幻觉。

Result: 实验表明,该方法与专家判断的契合度优于基线模型,在保持计算效率的同时提升了分析准确性和上下文感知能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LLM与人类专业知识相结合可有效支持公共部门的多语言客户服务反馈分析,为开发负责任的AI系统提供参考,有助于提升服务质量、响应速度和公共服务公信力。

Abstract: Enhancing the analysis of service feedback is essential for public sector organizations, particularly tax administrations, where trust and compliance depend on fair and effective service delivery. As feedback volumes grow, identifying emerging service quality issues and potential disparities across diverse populations becomes increasingly challenging. Traditional approaches often rely on manual review or static expert-defined indicators, limiting scalability and the ability to capture complex patterns in textual feedback.
  This paper presents a novel methodology that integrates large language models (LLMs), statistical techniques, and human-AI collaboration to improve multilingual customer feedback analysis. The primary objective is to detect emerging service quality topics that may also reveal potential inequities in service delivery. Our framework combines fine-tuned, quantized LLMs with expert oversight to produce accurate, computationally efficient, and context-aware analyses.
  The proposed approach was evaluated using similarity analysis and assessments from experienced tax officers, demonstrating stronger alignment with expert judgments than baseline models. By incorporating a human-in-the-loop framework, the methodology reduces LLM fabrication while improving the reliability and relevance of generated insights.
  The results demonstrate the practicality of combining LLMs with human expertise to support scalable, evidence-based decision-making in public sector organizations.
